An aborted Host journal operation could exit drain before Adapter terminal consumption; outer Host turn became cancelled and cancelTurn previously drained only public running/waiting status, leaving terminal queue that prevented restore. Minimal fix records captured run terminal consumption immediately, cancelTurn awaits agent cancel_turn and captured Host promise then drains that exact started run if terminal not yet consumed regardless public turn status; final cancellation persists empty assistant and interrupts captured member journal. Same readonly Su instance reused with current safe checkpoint; Human preserved, partial not persisted or replayed, old authority unchanged. Diagnostic output finite stages prepare_memory/restore/start/drain/next_event/finalize and whitelisted error types/codes only, never error raw text/history/key. cancel repair completed within5 assigned files, no Adapter production edits or scope expansion. Actual diagnosis corrected prior read-only inference: Host mapped restore TypeError to pi_run_failed; fixed host-a305e17c logs customer-stage:restore:TypeError:conversation_not_idle and first cancelled drain stale_action. An aborted Host journal operation could exit drain before Adapter terminal consumption; outer Host turn became cancelled and cancelTurn previously drained only public running/waiting status, leaving terminal queue that prevented restore. Minimal fix records captured run terminal consumption immediately, cancelTurn awaits agent cancel_turn and captured Host promise then drains that exact started run if terminal not yet consumed regardless public turn status; final cancellation persists empty assistant and interrupts captured member journal. Same readonly Su instance reused with current safe checkpoint; Human preserved, partial not persisted or replayed, old authority unchanged. Diagnostic output finite stages prepare_memory/restore/start/drain/next_event/finalize and whitelisted error types/codes only, never error raw text/history/key. cancel repair completed within5 assigned files, no Adapter production edits or scope expansion. Actual diagnosis corrected prior read-only inference: Host mapped restore TypeError to pi_run_failed; fixed host-a305e17c logs customer-stage:restore:TypeError:conversation_not_idle and first cancelled drain stale_action. An aborted Host journal operation could exit drain before Adapter terminal consumption; outer Host turn became cancelled and cancelTurn previously drained only public running/waiting status, leaving terminal queue that prevented restore. Minimal fix records captured run terminal consumption immediately, cancelTurn awaits agent cancel_turn and captured Host promise then drains that exact started run if terminal not yet consumed regardless public turn status; final cancellation persists empty assistant and interrupts captured member journal. Same readonly Su instance reused with current safe checkpoint; Human preserved, partial not persisted or replayed, old authority unchanged. Diagnostic output finite stages prepare_memory/restore/start/drain/next_event/finalize and whitelisted error types/codes only, never error raw text/history/key.
